            <h2 class="hidden md:flex font-bold text-4xl line-height-5xl font-64">Why Jotform Enterprise?</h2>

            <p>Specifically tailored for organizations looking to streamline and automate, the Jotform Enterprise platform offers:</p>

            <ul class="list-disc list-inside">

              <li>Intuitive drag and drop functionality</li>

              <li>20,000+ form templates</li>

              <li>Powerful integrations</li>

              <li>E-signatures, approvals, and reporting</li>

              <li>World-class support</li>

            </ul>

                <img loading="lazy" class="w-full h-auto -mb-1 challenges__img lazyloaded" src="https://cdn.jotfor.ms/image/r/aHR0cHM6Ly9jZG4uam90Zm9yLm1zL3AvcGFydG5lcnNoaXAvYXNzZXRzL2ltZy1taW4vcmVzZWxsZXIvZW1iZWQvY2hhbGxlbmdlcy5wbmc=/challenges.png?w=1024&amp;t=1" data-src="https://cdn.jotfor.ms/image/r/aHR0cHM6Ly9jZG4uam90Zm9yLm1zL3AvcGFydG5lcnNoaXAvYXNzZXRzL2ltZy1taW4vcmVzZWxsZXIvZW1iZWQvY2hhbGxlbmdlcy5wbmc=/challenges.png?w=1024&amp;t=1" alt="Challenges" width="848" height="529">

            </picture>            </div>

            <div class="swiper-slide">

              <div class="flex flex-col gap-2 md:gap-6 bg-navy-25 radius-lg p-4 md:p-10 line-height-xl color-navy-500 text-center md:text-left h-full">

                <p class="font-bold text-md md:text-2xl">Too hard to use</p>

                <p class="text-md md:text-xl line-height-xl md:line-height-3xl">Some require coding, others have cryptic old interfaces — taking too much time for even the simplest form.</p>

              </div>

            </div>

            <div class="swiper-slide">

              <div class="flex flex-col gap-2 md:gap-6 bg-navy-25 radius-lg p-4 md:p-10 line-height-xl color-navy-500 text-center md:text-left h-full">

                <p class="font-bold text-md md:text-2xl">Don’t include templates</p>

                <p class="text-md md:text-xl line-height-xl md:line-height-3xl">Many require you to build a form from scratch, without any premade templates or design assets.</p>

              </div>

            </div>

            <div class="swiper-slide">

              <div class="flex flex-col gap-2 md:gap-6 bg-navy-25 radius-lg p-4 md:p-10 line-height-xl color-navy-500 text-center md:text-left h-full">

                <p class="font-bold text-md md:text-2xl">Can’t integrate with “your” application</p>

                <p class="text-md md:text-xl line-height-xl md:line-height-3xl">Some form builders provide integration—but only with the most common apps.</p>

              </div>

            </div>

            <div class="swiper-slide">

              <div class="flex flex-col gap-2 md:gap-6 bg-navy-25 radius-lg p-4 md:p-10 line-height-xl color-navy-500 text-center md:text-left h-full">

                <p class="font-bold text-md md:text-2xl">Pay for forms add-ons</p>

                <p class="text-md md:text-xl line-height-xl md:line-height-3xl">If you want to convert your forms to PDF, get an e-signature, or need to route your form data for approvals you need additional programs.</p>

              </div>

            </div>

            <div class="swiper-slide">

              <div class="flex flex-col gap-2 md:gap-6 bg-navy-25 radius-lg p-4 md:p-10 line-height-xl color-navy-500 text-center md:text-left h-full">

                <p class="font-bold text-md md:text-2xl">Poor support</p>

                <p class="text-md md:text-xl line-height-xl md:line-height-3xl">Many have poor documentation, don’t provide phone or live chat when you need it — and long queues even if they have it.</p>

              </div>

            </div>

          </div>

          </div>

          <h3 class="text-center color-white text-5xl line-height-6xl font-bold ">The Big Question</h3>

          <p class="text-center color-white text-md line-height-xl">How can you streamline your form-building process, ensure sleek and modern design, easily integrate with other tools, receive timely support, and access all features without breaking the bank?</p>

    <button class="video-close js-video-close locale-data-aria-label" aria-label="Close Button">

        <div class="sr-only">Close</div>

    <p class="ytTModalTitle"></p>

    <iframe id="ytplayer" title="Youtube Embed" width="1200" height="675" src="" frameborder="0" allow="accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture" allowfullscreen="allowfullscreen" mozallowfullscreen="mozallowfullscreen" msallowfullscreen="msallowfullscreen" oallowfullscreen="oallowfullscreen" webkitallowfullscreen="webkitallowfullscreen"></iframe>

</div>


<script>

    function youtubeLB(videoID = false) {

      let videoPlayer = document.querySelector("#videoPlayer");

      var html = document.querySelector("html"),

        body = document.querySelector("body");

    

      if (videoPlayer !== null) {

        var closeBtn = videoPlayer.querySelector(".js-video-close");

        videoPlayer.player = videoPlayer.querySelector("#ytplayer");

    

        if (videoID) {

          videoPlayer.anchors = videoID;

          openPlayer(videoID);

        } else {

          videoPlayer.anchors = body.querySelectorAll("[data-yt-video-id]");

          Array.prototype.slice.call(videoPlayer.anchors).map(function (el) {

            el.addEventListener("click", function (e) {

              e.preventDefault();

              if (el.dataset.ytTitle) {

                document.querySelector(".ytTModalTitle").innerText = el.dataset.ytTitle;

              }

              openPlayer(el.getAttribute("data-yt-video-id"));

            });

          });

        }

    

    

        videoPlayer.addEventListener("click", function (e) {

          videoPlayer.player.src = "";

          html.classList.remove("videoActive");

        });

    

        closeBtn.addEventListener("click", function (e) {

          videoPlayer.player.src = "";

          html.classList.remove("videoActive");

        });

    

        document.addEventListener("keydown", function (e) {

          if (html.classList.contains("videoActive") && e.keyCode === 27) {

            videoPlayer.player.src = "";

            html.classList.remove("videoActive");

          }

        });

    

        function openPlayer(id) {

          videoPlayer.player.src = "https://www.youtube.com/embed/" + id + "?autoplay=1&controls=1&fs=1&playsinline=1&color=white";

          videoPlayer.player.addEventListener("load", function handler(e) {

            html.classList.add("videoActive");

            this.removeEventListener(e.type, handler);

          });

        }

      }

    }


    youtubeLB();

</script>  </main>
